Off-policy methods such as supervised fine-tuning, on the other hand, can leverage external knowledge beyond the base model's capabilities, but may suffer from large distribution shift. The key challenge is thus to expand exploration without sacrificing learnability. In this work, we introduce Minimal Intervention Reinforcement Learning (MInTRL), which expands the exploration frontier through sparse, local interventions in otherwise on-policy rollouts. During generation, a judge-intervention policy periodically reviews the current policy's output, replaces erroneous suffixes with short corrections, and immediately returns control to the policy. During training, MInTRL adopts a sequence-level advantage-regression objective that eliminates the need for importance sampling. We show that sparse, local interventions can substantially improve coverage beyond finite-budget on-policy sampling while preserving the overall on-policy nature of the resulting trajectories. Across math and code benchmarks, MInTRL consistently outperforms standard on-policy and off-policy baselines. Ablations show that MInTRL remains effective with self-intervention and across different judge policies, while performance peaks at moderate intervention intensity, highlighting the importance of intervening minimally. These results establish minimal intervention as an effective paradigm for enhancing on-policy RL.

</details>


### [177] [Granularity-Adaptive Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon LLM Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12424)
*Taoran Liang,Yang Liu,Shang Luo,Yingguang Yang,Rongrong Zhang,Yingzong Min,Yulin Huang,Jianshen Zhang,Yongzhi Qi,Kefu Xu,Congjing Ran,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 GACA，一种基于不确定性（NLL）自适应调节步级与回合级优势混合权重的无评论家估计器，在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上于 1.5B 和 7B 规模超越 GRPO 与 GiGPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务中奖励稀疏，GRPO 等群体相对方法只广播轨迹级标量，无法定位关键决策；GiGPO 虽引入步级信号，却用固定权重混合步级与回合级估计，对关键分叉决策和常规确定性转移投入相同分辨率。作者认为合理分辨率应依赖状态，即不确定性。

Method: GACA 以每步自身 rollout 已记录的负对数似然（NLL）作为不确定性/关键性代理，按步打分，并用随该分数增长的逐步骤权�重混合步级与回合级优势：NLL 高于均值时更偏向细粒度信号，低于均值时更偏向回合级信号。理论上给出所实现混合的精确风险分解，证明在小幅调制且方向正对齐时可优于固定混合；另有条件性结果用期望 NLL 界定局部动作价值变化，并通过误差投影分析刻画混合何时优于标量不确定性重加权。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 与 WebShop 上，GACA 在 1.5B 和 7B 两种规模下均提升了任务成功率，优于 GRPO 和 GiGPO。

Conclusion: 优势混合的粒度应随状态不确定性自适应变化；以 NLL 为关键性代理的逐步骤加权能在不引入评论家的前提下改善长时程语言模型智能体的强化学习效果，理论分析支持其相对固定混合与标量重加权的增益，并获实验验证。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is now the standard way to train large language model agents on long-horizon tasks, where dozens of interdependent actions precede a single sparse reward. Critic-free, group-relative methods such as GRPO suit this regime, but they broadcast one trajectory-level scalar to every step and cannot say which decision drove the outcome. GiGPO recovers a step-level signal by grouping time steps that share an anchor state, yet it merges the step- and episode-level estimates under one fixed weight, spending the same resolution on a pivotal branching decision as on a routine, near-deterministic transition. We argue that the right resolution is state-dependent, and propose GACA, a critic-free estimator whose granularity follows an uncertainty-based criticality proxy. GACA scores every step by the negative log-likelihood its own rollout already records, then blends the two advantages with a per-step weight that grows with that score, so the gradient places more weight on the fine-grained signal at above-average NLL and on the episode-level signal below it. We derive an exact risk decomposition for the implemented mixture and show that sufficiently small modulation improves on fixed mixing under positive directional alignment. A separate conditional result bounds local action-value variation using expected NLL, while an error-projection analysis characterizes when mixing adds value beyond scalar uncertainty reweighting. On ALFWorld and WebShop, GACA improves task success over GRPO and GiGPO at both 1.5B and 7B scales.

</details>


### [178] [Observation-Anchored Selective Assimilation for Longitudinal Tumor-State Proxy Forecasting in Post-Treatment Glioma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12435)
*Yeonjae Jung,Minwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将胶质瘤治疗后MRI的纵向预测建模为"观测锚定的数字孪生更新"，采用SegMamba单步预测器结合观测锚定选择性同化（OASA）策略，在120组患者级三元组上进行评估。OASA在保持与持久化方法相当的Dice(τ=0.2)的同时，在τ=0.5上数值更高；校准虽提升Dice但主要源于近阈值支撑校准，而非真正提升生物学预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶质瘤治疗后MRI提供系列观察，可用于更新患者特异性的肿瘤状态代理估计，但肿瘤形态与演变轨迹的多变性使预测困难。作者希望将预测问题重新定义为"观测感知"的数字孪生状态更新问题，利用中间观测锚定患者特异性状态以改善预测。

Method: 在203名患者、594个随访时间点中，按预设"无新治疗"标准从236个候选三元组中保留120个，并在患者层面划分为81/24/15的训练/验证/测试集。每个时间点用源自MRI病灶标签的连续体素级肿瘤状态代理图（[0,1]）表示。采用基于SegMamba的单步预测器从多模态源状态张量生成更新提议。OASA将观测到的中间代理作为状态锚点，通过验证集选择的层级病例级规则和体素级软门控选择性应用更新。比较了初始扫描预测、无同化 rollout、最近观测持久化、直接预测、OASA、OASA+校准及形态学膨胀等方法，检查点、OASA规则和校准阈值仅用验证数据选择。

Result: 在15组留出测试三元组、三个随机种子上：OASA在τ=0.2的Dice与持久化相当（0.6071±0.0025 vs. 0.6070），在τ=0.5上数值更高（0.4269±0.0079 vs. 0.3981），RMSE略有增加。校准将τ=0.2的Dice提升至0.6178±0.0025，假阳性支撑增加（11,836→18,663），假阴性支撑减少（22,107→17,536）。作者指出这反映的是近阈值支撑校准，而非生物学预测能力的提升。

Conclusion: 观测锚定的选择性同化（OASA）能在保持与持久化基线相当性能的同时，在更严格的阈值下提供更高的Dice，是一种可行的观测感知数字孪生更新策略。校准带来的增益主要源于近阈值支撑的分布调整，而非真正提升了对肿瘤演变的生物学预测能力，提示应谨慎解读此类指标改善。代码已公开。

Abstract: Post-treatment MRI in patients with glioma provides serial observations for updating patient-specific tumor-state proxy estimates, but variable appearances and trajectories complicate forecasting. We formulate forecasting as an observation-aware digital-twin update in which an intermediate observation anchors the patient-specific state. Among 203 patients and 594 follow-up time points, a predefined no-new-treatment criterion retained 120 of 236 candidate triplets, split into 81/24/15 training/validation/test triplets at the patient level. Each time point was represented by a continuous voxel-wise tumor-state proxy map in [0,1] derived from MRI lesion labels. A SegMamba-based single-step forecaster predicted update proposals from multimodal source-state tensors. Observation-Anchored Selective Assimilation (OASA) retained the observed intermediate proxy as the state anchor and selectively applied updates through a validation-selected tiered case-level rule and voxel-wise soft gate. We compared initial-scan forecasting, rollout without assimilation, latest-observation persistence, direct prediction, OASA, OASA + calibration, and morphological dilation. Checkpoints, OASA rules, and calibration thresholds were selected using validation data only. Across three seeds on 15 held-out test triplets, OASA maintained Dice at $τ$ = 0.2 comparable to persistence (0.6071 $\pm$ 0.0025 vs. 0.6070) while yielding numerically higher Dice at $τ$ = 0.5 (0.4269 $\pm$ 0.0079 vs. 0.3981), with a small RMSE increase. Calibration increased Dice at $τ$ = 0.2 to 0.6178 $\pm$ 0.0025, increased false-positive (FP) support (11,836$\rightarrow$18,663), and reduced false-negative (FN) support (22,107$\rightarrow$17,536). This reflects near-threshold support calibration rather than improved biological predictive capability. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/jsudg436/longitudinal-proxy-forecasting.

</details>


### [179] [Beyond the Query: Do Retrieval Signals Improve Adaptive Multimodal RAG Routing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12437)
*Qiaomu Li,Qiuyuan Zhang,Nong Ming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑自适应RAG中检索时信号的路由价值：在文档、音频、视频RAG中，控制变量对比"仅查询"与"查询+检索"路由器，发现检索信号相对仅查询基线并无可靠的路由提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应RAG常用检索时信号（retrieval-time signals）来决定是否执行额外检索、重排序或多模态步骤。但作者想知道：当查询本身已知时，这些检索信号是否还能带来额外的路由价值。现有做法往往默认其有效，缺乏严格的对照验证。

Method: 在文档、音频、视频三类RAG任务上，构建匹配的"仅查询"路由器与"查询+检索信号"路由器，并固定可选动作、路由器家族、训练流程与评估方式，在留出的最终评估集上对比两者的路由表现。

Result: 在最终评估中，加入所测试的检索信号并未比仅查询基线产生可靠的路由改进。部分检索信号虽与"后续步骤是否有帮助"存在关联，但这种可预测性并不能稳定转化为更好的RUN/SKIP决策。

Conclusion: 主要结论是方法论层面的：除非检索状态特征能胜过匹配的仅查询对照，否则不应赋予其路由价值。结果并非说明路由或检索状态普遍无用，而是强调检索信号的增量价值必须被证明，而非默认假设。

Abstract: Adaptive RAG often uses retrieval-time signals to decide whether another retrieval, reranking, or multimodal step should run. We ask whether these signals add routing value once the query itself is already known. Across document, audio, and video RAG, we compare matched query-only and query+retrieval routers while holding the optional actions, router family, training procedure, and evaluation fixed. On the held-out final evaluation, adding the tested retrieval signals does not produce a reliable routing improvement over the query-only baseline. Some retrieval signals are associated with whether a later step will help, but that predictability does not consistently lead to bet- ter RUN/SKIP decisions. The main lesson is therefore methodological: retrieval-state features should not be credited with routing value unless they improve over a matched query-only control. Our results do not show that routing or retrieval state is generally useless; they show that the incremental value of retrieval signals must be demonstrated rather than assumed.

</details>


### [180] [3D Digital Twin Visualization of Multiclass GRF-Based Gait Disorder Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12442)
*Nayoung Son,Minwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一套集成框架，利用双侧地面反作用力(GRF)与压力中心(COP)信号，实现健康与多种肌骨损伤步态的自动分类、可解释性分析及3D可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化步态分析需要同时具备准确的分类能力和可解释的输出，以便提升模型透明度并支持样本级别的检查。

Method: 信号在支撑相内归一化，并使用训练集统计量进行标准化；构建分类模型区分健康步态与多个肌骨损伤组；采用类特定ε-LRP解释正负贡献，覆盖左右两侧、多个信号分量及不同支撑相；将处理后的GRF信号与模型预测同步至基于Blender的3D可视化中。

Result: 在会话级划分下，验证准确率为99.00%，测试准确率为90.07%。

Conclusion: 该框架整合了分类、可解释性与3D可视化，提升了模型透明度；源代码已开源。

Abstract: Automated gait analysis requires accurate classification and interpretable outputs. We propose an integrated framework for classifying healthy gait and multiple musculoskeletal impairment groups using bilateral ground reaction force (GRF) and center-of-pressure (COP) signals. The signals were normalized over the stance phase and standardized using training-set statistics. The model achieved a validation accuracy of 99.00\% and a test accuracy of 90.07\% under a session-level split. Class-specific $ε$-LRP identified positive and negative contributions across both sides, multiple signal components, and different stance phases. Separately, the processed GRF signals and model predictions were synchronized within a Blender-based 3D visualization, enabling sample-level inspection of gait trials and classification results. The proposed framework integrates classification, explainability, and 3D visualization to improve model transparency. The source code is available in the following repository: https://github.com/nyoico/grf-gait-3d-visualization.git

</details>


### [181] [SAGE-Loop: Reliable Closed-Loop LLM-Driven AutoML with Trial-and-Correction and Adaptive Ensembling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12455)
*Junquan Gu,Shibo Cui,Xiangfeng Luo,Hang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SAGE-Loop，一个可靠的闭环自适应 LLM 驱动 AutoML 框架，通过多轮生成与验证实现“试错-修复”，并自适应选择集成策略，统一“如何生成模型”与“如何使用模型”。在 20 个公开数据集上于分类、回归、聚类任务中一致提升性能与稳定性，并能从执行失败中恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AutoML（尤其是引入 LLM 后）存在单向流程问题：中间失败通常被终止或绕过，执行时缺乏即时反馈与自适应优化，一旦运行进入次优或失败状态便缺少过程级纠错机制；同时固定范式多强化模型生成，却使集成决策保持静态，削弱了执行可靠性与结构化多样性的可控使用。因此需要闭环的“试错-修正-改进”能力以及基于证据的模型多样性利用。

Method: 提出 SAGE-Loop：可靠的闭环、自适应、LLM 驱动 AutoML 框架。其核心是执行多轮生成与验证以实现 trial-and-repair（试错与修复），并自适应地在监督与无监督任务中选择集成策略，从而统一模型生成与模型使用。

Result: 在 20 个公开数据集上，SAGE-Loop 在分类、回归和聚类任务中持续提升性能与稳定性；额外结果表明其具备从执行失败中恢复的能力，并保持稳健的流水线行为。

Conclusion: SAGE-Loop 通过闭环自适应机制弥补了 LLM 驱动 AutoML 在过程级纠错与集成决策动态化方面的不足，验证了闭环“试错-修复”与证据化多样性利用在可靠性与性能上的有效性。

Abstract: Automated machine learning (AutoML) is reshaping data-driven science and industrial practice, and as large language models are introduced into AutoML, pipeline reliability becomes as important as automation efficiency. However, existing AutoML still struggles to realize instant feedback and adaptive optimization during execution, so once a run drifts into a suboptimal or failed state, it lacks a process-level correction mechanism. The fundamental pathology lies in its one-way pipeline: intermediate failures are typically terminated or bypassed, while fixed paradigms often strengthen model generation but leave ensemble decisions static, weakening both execution reliability and the controlled use of structural diversity. This indicates that LLM-driven AutoML needs a closed-loop ability for trial-correction-improvement together with evidence-based use of model diversity. To this end, we propose SAGE-Loop, a reliable closed-loop, self-adaptive, LLM-driven AutoML framework that performs multi-round generation and validation for trial-and-repair, and adaptively selects ensemble strategies in both supervised and unsupervised tasks, thereby unifying how to generate with how to use models. Across 20 public datasets, SAGE-Loop consistently improves performance and stability on classification, regression, and clustering tasks. Additional results further show its ability to recover from execution failures and maintain robust pipeline behavior.

</details>


### [182] [A Differentially Private Federated Proximal Optimization Framework for Customer Churn Prediction in Heterogeneous Federated Telecom Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12470)
*Joydeb Kumar Sana,Subrata Chakraborty,M M Manjurul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于差分隐私的联邦近端优化框架（DP-FedProx），用于电信客户流失预测，在保护数据隐私的同时取得与集中式模型相近的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信行业客户流失预测多依赖集中式机器学习，需将客户数据汇聚到中心存储库，带来隐私泄露风险并可能违反数据保护法规。联邦学习可让多个运营商在不共享原始数据的前提下协同训练，但真实客户数据常呈异构（non-IID）分布，会削弱标准联邦学习性能，且模型还面临隐私攻击威胁。

Method: 提出差分隐私联邦近端优化框架（DP-FedProx），将差分隐私（DP）与联邦近端优化（FedProx）结合，以应对non-IID数据与隐私攻击问题。在两个公开电信流失数据集上，训练了FedAvg、DP-FedAvg、FedProx及所提DP-FedProx，并以多个集中式与本地模型作为基线，采用七种常用评价指标进行评估，同时用SHAP进行可解释性分析。

Result: 基于FedProx的模型性能一致优于基于FedAvg的模型。相比最优集中式模型，DP-FedProx在提供隐私保障的同时仅损失少量准确率，预测性能具有竞争力。SHAP分析显示DP-FedProx优先关注收入组相关特征。

Conclusion: 所提DP-FedProx框架在预测性能与数据隐私保护之间实现了实用的平衡，适合在隐私敏感的电信客户流失预测场景中应用。

Abstract: Customer churn is one of the major issues in the telecommunication industry. To predict customer churn, conventional centralized machine learning approaches have been widely used. This centralized approach requires customer data to be stored in a central repository, which raises privacy concerns and may violate data protection regulations. Federated learning addresses this problem by allowing multiple telecom operators to collaboratively train a global model without transferring their raw customer data. However, real-world customer data are often heterogeneous (non-IID), which may negatively affect the performance of standard federated learning. Trained models can also suffer from privacy attacks. To address those issues, we propose a Differentially Private (DP) based Federated Proximal optimization (FedProx) framework. All experiments were performed on two publicly available telecom churn datasets. We trained Federated Averaging (FedAvg), DP-FedAvg, FedProx, and the proposed DP-FedProx framework. For baseline comparison, we also used several centralized and local models. To evaluate the models, we employed seven widely used evaluation metrics. The experimental results show that the FedProx based models consistently outperform the FedAvg based models. Compared with the best centralized model, the proposed DP-FedProx framework achieves competitive prediction performance with only a small reduction in accuracy while providing privacy guarantees. To explain our model, we conducted SHAP analysis which shows that DP-FedProx method priorities revenue group features. These results indicate that the proposed DP-FedProx framework provides a practical balance between prediction performance and data privacy protection.

</details>


### [183] [Temporal Recurrence Favors Fewer Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12531)
*Ivan Anokhin,Johan Obando-Ceron,Irina Rish,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究时序递归是否减少每步内部深度需求，将架构设计视为计算分配问题，发现递归使最优分配倾向于更少层数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序递归已能跨时间步提供序列计算，作者想探究：当递归提供顺序计算后，每一步内部还需要多少深度。不同于已有工作仅表明递归使浅层模型具竞争力，本文将问题形式化为计算分配问题。

Method: 在多个计算预算下，变化步内深度（层数）、专家宽度以及每层并行专家数量，比较递归与非递归模型在每步计算量近似匹配时的最佳观测分配。

Result: 在 Sokoban 和自回归 FineWeb 语言建模任务上，时序递归将最佳观测计算分配显著移向更少层数，同时达到相当或更优的性能。

Conclusion: 时序递归可替代部分步内深度，使计算分配转向更少层数；即递归提供了顺序计算能力后，每步所需深度可大幅减少而不损失性能。

Abstract: In streaming tasks, recurrent models can carry latent computation across time, allowing each update to build on representations produced earlier. This raises a basic question: once temporal recurrence provides sequential computation across steps, how much depth is still needed within each step? Prior work has shown that recurrence can make shallow models competitive. We instead study this question as a compute-allocation problem, varying within-step depth, expert width, and the number of parallel experts per layer across several compute budgets. For each budget, we compare the best observed recurrent and non-recurrent allocations and the performance they achieve under approximately matched per-step computation. Across Sokoban and autoregressive FineWeb language modeling, we find that temporal recurrence shifts the best observed compute allocation toward substantially fewer layers, with comparable or better performance.

</details>


### [184] [$\text{GSF-}χ$: Global Stereochemical Fields for Chiral Graph Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12532)
*Jiaqing Xie,Yuxin Wang,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出GSF-χ图变换器，利用立体单元调制所有成对交互，并通过Chiral-RoPE和C₂投影处理手性分子，性能优于最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对映异构体共享原子、键和成对距离，但在手性环境中行为不同，因此分子编码器必须尊重原子重标记和真旋转，同时又不能对反射"失明"。

Method: 提出GSF-χ图变换器：每个中心或轴向立体单元在所有原子上产生反射偶相位场，手性伪标量χ为潜在query-key块上的相对旋转设定方向，形成反射时会反转的Chiral-RoPE；用C₂投影将镜偶ECD峰数和位置与镜奇峰符号分离；并证明该算子的偶-奇分解及注释反转、置换和单位阶恒等式。

Result: GSF-χ在所有中心ECD输出上领先，轴向Rotation和Symbol分别比最强基线提升12.6%和7.9%；同等预算控制表明Rotation优势来自全局有符号支持而非参数量或边数；C₂投影在完整监督下以少量原始精度损失实现精确对映体对一致性，在镜像监督稀缺时具有预测能力。

Conclusion: GSF-χ通过立体单元调制成对交互、Chiral-RoPE和C₂投影，有效处理手性分子编码，并在ECD相关任务上超越基线，同时其理论性质通过属性测试和坐标反射审计得到验证。

Abstract: Enantiomers share atoms, bonds, and pairwise distances yet can behave differently in chiral environments, so molecular encoders must respect atom relabelings and proper rotations without becoming blind to reflection. We introduce GSF-$χ$, a graph transformer in which stereogenic units modulate all pairwise interactions rather than single out one atom as special. Each central or axial stereogenic unit creates a reflection-even phase field over all atoms, a handedness pseudoscalar $χ$ sets the direction of a relative rotation on latent query--key blocks, giving a \textbf{Chiral-RoPE} that reflection inverts rather than leaves fixed. A $C_2$ projection separates mirror-even ECD peak counts and positions from mirror-odd peak signs. We prove the operator's even--odd decomposition and its annotation-inversion, permutation, and unit-order identities under explicit canonical-role conditions; property tests and a coordinate-reflection audit verify the laws end to end. GSF-$χ$ leads every central-ECD output and improves axial Rotation and Symbol by $12.6\%$ and $7.9\%$ over the strongest baseline. Equal-budget controls attribute the Rotation advantage to global signed support rather than parameter or edge count; the $C_2$ projection yields exact enantiomer-pair consistency at a small raw-accuracy cost under complete supervision and becomes predictive when mirror supervision is scarce.

</details>


### [185] [Quality-Constrained Routing over a Fixed Pool of Quantized Mixture-of-Experts Instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12550)
*Zhenghong Huang,Hongfan Wu,Jiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在固定常驻实例池边界内研究量化MoE服务的请求路由问题，提出FWP（脆弱性加权困惑度）来预测请求级量化风险，并通过窗口级线性规划实现质量约束下的吞吐最大化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化MoE可将一个基础模型预物化为多个不同位宽的实例，但量化损伤随请求和位宽差异极大。由于实例物化和副本数消耗内存且重配置缓慢，需将其视为上游供给决策，从而聚焦于固定常驻池内的路由优化问题。

Method: 提出FWP（Fragility-Weighted Perplexity），在参考实例prefill上由prompt token计算并校准到候选实例的退化程度；其基础是精确的双专家亲和-脆弱性分解与条件多层top-k展开，显式保留偏置、交互、路由变化、可分性和高阶项。基于校准风险，用窗口级线性规划得到带符号的缩减奖励分数，该分数在最优价格与原始可行平局分配下与LP最优解KKT一致。

Result: 在88条扩展Qwen提示上，对全部6,144个专家块量化得到完整W2、W3、W4实例，平均ΔNLL分别为0.9437、0.1832、0.0513。在相同总体与τ=0.1513下，FWP分配达到1.284×的离线基于模型倍率，而请求无关混合为1.253×、静态W4为1.000×，FWP相对增益为2.5%。

Conclusion: 在固定实例池内，基于FWP的请求级风险预测与线性规划路由能够显著优于请求无关混合和静态配置，在满足类别级质量退化预算的同时提升吞吐。

Abstract: Quantized Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) services can hold several pre-materialized instances of one base model, but quantization damage varies sharply across requests and bitwidths. Because instance materialization and replica counts consume memory and require slow reconfiguration, we treat them as upstream provisioning decisions and study routing within a fixed resident pool. Within this fixed-pool boundary, we route each request to maximize modeled throughput under a class-level expected quality-degradation budget and measured instance capacities. To predict this request-specific risk, we introduce FWP (Fragility-Weighted Perplexity), computed from prompt tokens on a reference-instance prefill and calibrated to candidate-instance degradation. Underlying FWP is an exact two-expert affinity--fragility decomposition and a conditional multi-layer top-$k$ expansion whose bias, interaction, route-change, separability, and higher-order terms remain explicit. Using these calibrated risks, a window-level linear program yields a signed reduced-reward score that is KKT-consistent with the LP optimum under optimal prices and primal-feasible tie allocation. On 88 extended Qwen prompts, complete W2, W3, and W4 instances quantizing all 6,144 expert blocks incur mean $Δ$NLL of $0.9437$, $0.1832$, and $0.0513$. Under the same population and $τ=0.1513$, FWP allocation reaches a $1.284\times$ offline model-based multiplier versus $1.253\times$ for request-agnostic mixing and $1.000\times$ for static W4, an incremental $2.5\%$ relative FWP gain.

</details>


### [186] [TokenMapper: A Step Toward Interoperable Speech Token Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12563)
*Tal Kozakov,Tal Rosenwein,Eliya Nachmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TokenMapper，一个方向感知框架，用于在离散域中直接进行异构语音分词器之间的 token 到 token 翻译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经音频编解码器将语音离散化为 token 序列，但不同模型的 token 空间在词表和码本结构上不一致，无法直接通信。跨语音系统传递信息通常需要先解码为波形再用第二个分词器重新编码，增加延迟且可能带来信息损失。

Method: TokenMapper 是一个方向感知的框架，支持结构不匹配的 token 空间相互映射，包括单码本与多码本表示之间的映射，并在共享的有效 token 率下进行。

Result: 在 GLM-4-Voice、MiMi 和 DualCodec 上实验显示一致的跨模型表现：翻译 WER 接近原生重建，绝对差异在 2.5–6.8%；TokenMapper 输出的人类 MOS 为 2.29 至 4.39，趋势与 UTMOS 一致；端到端延迟相比波形桥接降低 4.8–94.5%，每句最高可达 972 ms。

Conclusion: 该结果为跨模型语音 token 互操作性提供了实用的一步，无需中间波形重建。

Abstract: Neural audio codecs discretize speech into token sequences, but the resulting token spaces differ in vocabulary and codebook structure, preventing direct communication across models. This limitation affects applications such as conversational voice agents and speech to speech translation systems where multiple speech models must interact. As a result, transferring information between speech systems typically requires decoding to waveform audio and re-encoding with a second tokenizer, increasing latency and introducing potential information loss. To address these limitations, we present TokenMapper, a direction aware framework for direct token to token translation between heterogeneous speech tokenizers in the discrete domain. TokenMapper supports structurally mismatched token spaces, including mappings between single codebook and multi codebook representations, under a shared effective token rate. Experiments on GLM-4-Voice, MiMi and DualCodec show consistent cross model performance. Specifically, translation WER approaches native reconstructions within 2.5-6.8% absolute WER, human MOS for TokenMapper outputs ranges from 2.29 to 4.39, following the same direction level trends as UTMOS and end to end latency is reduced by 4.8-94.5% relative to waveform bridging, reaching up to 972 ms per utterance. These results provide a practical step toward cross model speech token interoperability without intermediate waveform reconstruction.

</details>


### [187] [SCOPE-OPSD: Fisher-Conditioned Privileged Subspaces for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12579)
*Yunmeng Chen,Kunyu Wang,Peihan Li,Yi Wang,Shuyin Xia,Yi Liu,Xinyong Cheng,Dehui Wang,Xiangyong Zhai,Yanxing Liu,Song Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SCOPE-OPSD，通过在冻结的低秩因子（由残差协方差与 LM-head Fisher 敏感度估计）上投影特权教师-学生残差，为 on-policy 自蒸馏增加第二个监督通道，并在 Qwen3 多尺寸模型上验证其稳定增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 on-policy 自蒸馏（OPSD）仅通过下一 token 概率传递监督信号，作者希望探究对齐后的最终层差异是否能作为有效的第二监督通道，并在不混淆其几何结构与辅助强度的情况下对其进行检验。

Method: SCOPE-OPSD 将特权教师-学生残差投影到冻结的 rank-64 因子上，该因子由残差协方差与语言模型头 Fisher 敏感度估计得到；复用 OPSD 已有的前向计算，不增加 rollout 或推理模块。同时设置匹配的 Random 对照，保持结构化因子的秩与非零谱，并用逐臂梯度 RMS 校准，以隔离数据依赖方向的影响。

Result: 在 Qwen3-1.7B、4B 和 8B 的完整 25/50/75/100 步轨迹上，Structured 从未低于 Pure OPSD，在 12 个模型-检查点组合中有 11 个取得严格增益，4B 第 25 步完全持平；Structured 在 12 个组合中有 10 个超过匹配 Random。Qwen3-1.7B 第 75 步时，Structured 在两次独立训练重跑中均比匹配 Random 高出 1.39 Macro Avg@12；交叉拟合诊断显示其留存特权差距捕获能力是匹配随机方向的 4.40 倍。

Conclusion: 结果为短预算 OPSD 提供了一个紧凑的、Fisher 条件化的特权子空间方案，表明对齐最终层差异可作为有效的第二监督通道。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) scores student-generated prefixes with a solution-conditioned self-teacher, yet transfers supervision only through next-token probabilities. We ask whether the aligned final-layer discrepancy offers a useful second channel, and how to test that channel without confusing its geometry with auxiliary strength. SCOPE-OPSD projects the privileged teacher-student residual onto a frozen rank-64 factor estimated from residual covariance and language-model-head Fisher sensitivity. It reuses the forwards already required by OPSD and adds neither rollouts nor inference-time modules. A matched Random control preserves the structured factor's rank and nonzero spectrum and uses per-arm gradient-RMS calibration, isolating the effect of the data-dependent orientation. Across the complete 25/50/75/100-step trajectories for Qwen3-1.7B, 4B, and 8B, Structured is never below Pure OPSD, with strict gains in 11 of the 12 model-checkpoint combinations and an exact tie at 4B step 25. Structured also exceeds matched Random in 10 of the 12 combinations. At step 75 on Qwen3-1.7B, Structured exceeds matched Random by 1.39 Macro Avg@12 points in each of two independent training reruns. A cross-fitted diagnostic also shows 4.40 times greater held-out privileged-gap capture than the matched random orientation. The results support a compact, Fisher-conditioned privileged subspace for short-budget OPSD.

</details>


### [188] [Clustering-Based Balanced Sampling and Allocation with Data Parallelism for High-Performance Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12584)
*Hyunjin Kim,Youngeun Nam,Jaemin Han,Wonhyeok Choi,Jae-Gil Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CluSTER 提出一种面向多 GPU 数据并行指令微调的聚类感知平衡采样框架，通过梯度空间聚类与 DP 感知平衡分配构建精简数据集，在几乎不损失精度的前提下最多减少 69.6% 训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的指令微调数据往往规模庞大、冗余且类别不平衡，难以高效适配。朴素的扩大批次微调会反复采样过度代表的样本组，而对代表不足但信息量大的样本覆盖较弱，在多 GPU 数据并行（DP）场景下这一问题更为突出。

Method: 提出 CluSTER，一种用于数据并行指令微调的聚类感知平衡采样与高效数据缩减框架。它通过梯度空间聚类与 DP 感知的平衡分配来筛选具有代表性的精简数据集，实现簇级与工作节点级的双重覆盖，并通过加权更新保持原始数据分布。

Result: 在多个指令微调数据集上，与先前的采样和数据缩减方法相比，CluSTER 最多减少 69.6% 的训练时间，且几乎没有精度损失。

Conclusion: CluSTER 能在不损害模型质量的前提下减少冗余计算并提升训练稳定性，是一种有效的数据缩减与采样方案。代码已开源于 https://github.com/kaist-dmlab/CluSTER。

Abstract: Instruction-tuning datasets for large language models (LLMs) are often large, redundant, and imbalanced, limiting efficient adaptation. Naive large-batch fine-tuning repeatedly includes overrepresented sample groups while weakly covering underrepresented but informative ones, especially under data parallelism (DP) across multiple GPUs. We propose CluSTER, a Cluster-aware balanced Sampling framework for Training Efficient data Reduction in DP instruction tuning. CluSTER curates a representative reduced dataset through gradient-space clustering and DP-aware balanced allocation, ensuring dual-level coverage across clusters and workers, while preserving the original data distribution by weighted update. As a result, CluSTER reduces redundant computation and improves training stability without compromising model quality. Across multiple instruction-tuning datasets, CluSTER reduces training time by up to 69.6% with almost no accuracy loss compared to prior sampling and data reduction methods. Code is available at https://github.com/kaist-dmlab/CluSTER.

</details>


### [189] [Where Decoder Cosine Similarity Fails for SAE Feature Flow Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12591)
*Hendrik Droste,Christian Medeiros Adriano,Kathrin Korte,Holger Giese*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建"特征流转移图谱"，用稀疏自编码器刻画残差状态特征与MLP更新特征如何共同生成下游残差特征，并在Pythia-160M与Gemma-3-4B上做因果消融验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型通过微调、模型编辑与对齐不断演化，却仍需保留原有能力，因此理解支撑这些适配的内部计算变得关键。稀疏自编码器虽提供了可解释的特征字典，但状态特征与更新特征如何交互产生下游残差特征尚不清楚。

Method: 以MLP更新为切入点，构建三元组 s_k + u_j → t_ℓ 的转移图谱：残差状态特征与MLP更新特征联合预测目标残差特征，并通过消融解码后的更新特征来验证候选三元组。在Pythia-160M L7→L8（20M token）上全图谱验证；在Gemma-3-4B L21→L22（20M token）上仅对按排名前30,000的候选三元组做因果验证。

Result: Pythia中检出38,125个强消融效应转移，但88.0%的转移同时满足状态-目标与更新-目标解码器余弦相似度低于0.7。Gemma中53.6%的强效应三元组同样两项余弦相似度均低于0.7，方向与Pythia一致但更弱，因为更新-目标余弦能恢复许多最强效应，且该运行并非全图谱因果验证。

Conclusion: 特征流图谱可作为表征更新机制诊断工具，进而指导模型更新的引导手段；未来将在更多层、模型与SAE族上验证更复杂模式。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly adapted through fine-tuning, model editing, and alignment procedures while retaining previously acquired capabilities. Understanding the internal computations that support these adaptations is therefore becoming increasingly important for continual model evolution. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide interpretable feature dictionaries for residual-stream activations and sublayer outputs, but it remains unclear how state features and update features interact to produce downstream residual features. In this work, we focus on MLP updates as a first test case. We construct a transition atlas of triples $s_k + u_j \rightarrow t_\ell$, where a residual-state feature and an MLP-update feature jointly predict a target residual feature, and validate candidate triples by ablating the decoded update feature. In a 20M-token Pythia-160M $L_7 \rightarrow L_8$ run, we find 38,125 strong ablation-effect transitions, but 88.0% have both state-target and update-target decoder cosine similarity below 0.7. As a preliminary cross-model check, a run of 20M-token Gemma-3-4B $L_{21} \rightarrow L_{22}$ causally validates only the top 30,000 ranked candidate triples by ablating the decoded update feature, and 53.6% of strong-effect triples have both state-target and update-target decoder cosine similarity below 0.7. The Gemma result is directionally consistent with Pythia, but weaker, since update-target cosine recovers many of the strongest Gemma effects and the run is not a full-atlas causal validation. Ultimately, our results suggest that feature flow atlases can serve as diagnostics of representation-update mechanisms and thereby inform tools for steering model updates. Future work will validate more complex patterns across layers, models, and SAE families.

</details>


### [190] [Poisson-Corrector Complexity Bounds for Moreau--Yosida Unadjusted Langevin Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12594)
*Yuchen Xin,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究经典 Moreau–Yosida 未调整 Langevin 算法 (MYULA)，在强凸且梯度 Lipschitz 的 f 与凸且全局 Lipschitz 的 g 下，证明 MYULA 不变分布与 Moreau 平滑目标之间的 W2 距离为 O(h)+Õ(h^{3/4})，并由此得到 Õ(ε^{-4/3}) 的迭代复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标分布 π∝e^{-f-g} 中 g 仅为凸且 Lipschitz，导致分布可能非光滑；MYULA 通过 Moreau 平滑处理此类非光滑势函数，但其离散不变分布与平滑目标之间的定量误差及迭代复杂度仍需更精细的分析。

Method: 结合离散 Poisson 校正子、active-trace 估计，以及针对精确–Euler 两点曲率的共享噪声界，在步长条件 0<h(L_f+λ^{-1})≤c 下分析 MYULA 不变律与 Moreau 平滑目标间的 W2 误差。

Result: 证明 √m W2(π_λ, \widehatπ_{λ,h}) = O(h)+Õ(h^{3/4})，误差系数仅对 λ^{-1} 呈对数依赖；结合 Moreau 近似偏差，得到达到 √m W2(μ_N,π)≤ε 需 Õ(ε^{-4/3}) 次迭代（固定模型参数与初始化）。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了 MYULA 在非光滑复合目标下的非渐近 W2 收敛保证，并改进了迭代复杂度到 Õ(ε^{-4/3})，为 Moreau–Yosida Langevin 方法的精度–复杂度权衡提供了更优理论刻画。

Abstract: We study the classical Moreau--Yosida unadjusted Langevin algorithm (MYULA) for $π(\,\mathrm{d} x)\propto e^{-f(x)-g(x)}\,\mathrm{d} x$, where $f\in C^2(\mathbb{R}^d)$ is $m$-strongly convex with $L_f$-Lipschitz gradient and $g:\mathbb{R}^d\to\mathbb{R}$ is convex and globally $G$-Lipschitz. For the Moreau-smoothed target $π_λ$ and the MYULA invariant law $\widehatπ_{λ,h}$, we prove \[
  \sqrt m\,W_2(π_λ,\widehatπ_{λ,h})
  =O(h)+\widetilde O(h^{3/4}) \] under $0<h(L_f+λ^{-1})\le c$, with only logarithmic dependence on $λ^{-1}$ in the error coefficients. Combining this estimate with the Moreau approximation bias yields $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-4/3})$ iterations to achieve $\sqrt m\,W_2(μ_N,π)\le\varepsilon$, for fixed model parameters and initialization. The proof combines a discrete Poisson corrector with active-trace estimates and a shared-noise bound for the exact--Euler two-point curvature.

</details>


### [191] [SIMS: Scale-Invariant Merit-Function-Based Scalarization for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12599)
*Zebin Chen,Fei Xing,Yang Chen,Hua Liu,Andy HF Chow,Yuhua Qian,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出尺度不变的评价函数标量化方法SIMS，解决多任务学习中损失尺度差异导致的优化偏差，理论与实验均验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务学习需权衡多个竞争目标，常建模为多目标优化并通过标量化转为单目标。作者发现现有基于评价函数（merit function）的标量化方法对目标相对尺度敏感：实际任务损失常相差数个数量级，导致优化偏向尺度大的目标，尽管帕累托最优解对正比例缩放本应不变。

Method: 提出尺度不变的评价函数标量化（SIMS）。采用变换诱导的评价函数，将多目标优化转为对损失量级不变的单一目标；理论上证明尺度不变性要求唯一确定该变换为对数变换，并证明该变换保持弱帕累托最优性，且存在近似误差可控的光滑替代函数。

Result: 在代表性多任务基准上的大量实验表明，SIMS持续优于现有标量化方法，达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: SIMS通过对数变换实现尺度不变的标量化，既保证弱帕累托最优性又有可控近似的平滑形式，在多任务基准上取得最优表现，为多目标优化标量化提供了理论支撑与实用方案。

Abstract: Multi-task learning (MTL) requires navigating unavoidable trade-offs among competing objectives. This paradigm is frequently formulated as multi-objective optimization (MOO), where the scalarization is favored to reduce an MOO problem to a single objective. We empirically find that existing merit-function-based scalarization approaches are sensitive to the relative scales of different objectives in practical MTL, where task losses commonly differ by orders of magnitude. The optimization process often favors objectives with larger scales even though the underlying Pareto optimal solutions remains invariant to rescaling (i.e., multiplying an objective by a positive constant). To address this issue, we propose Scale-Invariant Merit-function-based Scalarization (SIMS) for MTL. Specifically, SIMS adopts a transformation-induced merit function to convert the MOO problem of MTL to a single objective that renders optimization invariant to the magnitudes of losses. Theoretically, we prove that the requirement for scale invariance uniquely determines this transformation to be logarithmic. We further show that this general transformation-induced merit function preserves weak Pareto optimality and admits a smooth surrogate with controllable approximation error. Extensive experiments on representative multi-task benchmarks demonstrate that SIMS consistently outperforms existing scalarization methods and achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [192] [Correlation-Guided Fast Machine Unlearning via Hessian Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12620)
*Ayushi Thakur,Ruchir Gupta,Amit Kumar Jaiswal,Prayag Tiwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出高效机器遗忘框架，通过识别相关数据点并应用闭式参数更新，实现82倍加速且保持模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络与分布式安全系统广泛采用机器学习，需选择性移除受损或对抗性训练数据的影响；GDPR等法规的"被遗忘权"也提出类似需求。但现有近似遗忘技术需对每个数据点反复进行昂贵的Hessian逆向量计算，在处理入侵检测、垃圾邮件过滤等场景中的多个相关请求时形成瓶颈，难以实际部署。

Method: 提出计算高效的遗忘框架：识别训练集中的相关数据点，应用理论推导的闭式参数更新规则；通过Hessian阻尼保证数值稳定性，并建立理论保证。

Result: 相较标准影响函数遗忘实现82倍墙钟加速；准确率比当前最优基线提升10^-2，同时保持模型效用。在七种数据集-架构组合（含大规模CIFAR-100与ResNet-50）上评估，遗忘效果优越：成员推理攻击成功率为0.660，tug-of-war分数为0.950。

Conclusion: 该框架在保证理论保证与数值稳定性的同时，显著提升遗忘效率与效果，适用于安全系统中多相关请求的删除场景，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The increasing adoption of machine learning in network and distributed security systems has created an urgent need for mechanisms that can selectively and efficiently remove the influence of specific training data to eliminate compromised or adversarial data points from production models. Privacy regulations such as GDPR's \emph{right to be forgotten} also pose similar requirements. However, existing approximate unlearning techniques remain computationally prohibitive for deployment in real-world security systems, as they require repeated expensive Hessian-inverse-vector computations for each data point removal, creating a bottleneck when processing multiple related requests in scenarios such as intrusion detection systems, spam filters, and threat intelligence platforms. Thus, we introduce a computationally efficient unlearning framework that identifies correlated data points in the training set and applies a theoretically derived closed-form parameter update rule, achieving an $82\times$ wall-clock speedup over standard influence function unlearning while preserving model utility with a $10^{-2}$ improvement in accuracy over state-of-the-art baselines. Our method establishes theoretical guarantees and ensures numerical stability through Hessian damping. Our evaluation across seven diverse dataset architecture combinations, including large-scale CIFAR-100 with ResNet-50, demonstrates superior forgetting effectiveness, with membership inference attack success rates of 0.660 and tug-of-war scores of 0.950.

</details>


### [193] [Geometric-to-Semantic Spherical Transfer Learning for Cortical Sulci Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12627)
*Saeb Tounsi,Joël Chavas,Pietro Gori,Vincent Frouin,Denis Rivière,Jean-François Mangin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出几何到语义的球面迁移学习框架，用约3万无标注数据预训练球面编码器，再通过软初始化拓扑先验注入器融合脑沟线语义信息，解决皮层表面标注稀缺导致的过拟合问题，平均Dice达0.77。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每半球需识别60多个依赖命名的脑沟，拓扑复杂，需要高容量模型；但专家标注极度稀缺（仅62名受试者），监督学习在此数据匮乏情况下严重过拟合，尤其是拓扑歧义高的可变小脑沟难以泛化。

Method: 先用大规模无标注数据（UK Biobank，约3万受试者）以局部优化策略预训练球面编码器，仅使用连续表面特征（曲率与深度）；下游标注任务引入提取的脑沟底（线）作为显式语义输入，通过软初始化的拓扑先验注入器将其整合进预训练主干，桥接从几何到语义的维度域差距并避免灾难性遗忘。

Result: 方法优于从零开始训练的完全监督基线，平均Dice达0.77；局部分析显示，自监督几何先验在可变脑沟与三级脑沟上带来最大性能提升（最高14.8%），证明学习皮层形状对识别其最稀有部分非常有益。

Conclusion: 几何预训练与语义先验注入结合可有效缓解标注稀缺下的过拟合，学习皮层形状有助于识别拓扑复杂且稀有的脑沟区域，验证了几何到语义迁移学习的有效性。

Abstract: Deep learning on cortical surfaces faces a dilemma: capturing the complex topology of over 60 nomenclature-dependent sulci per hemisphere requires high-capacity models, yet the extreme scarcity of expert annotations ($N=62$ subjects) inevitably causes overfitting. Standard supervised approaches fail to generalize in this data-scarce regime, particularly for variable and small sulci where topological ambiguity is high. To overcome this limitation, we introduce a Geometric-to-Semantic Spherical Transfer Learning framework.
  First, we leverage massive unlabeled data (UK Biobank, $\approx$30,000 subjects) to pre-train a spherical encoder using a locally-optimized strategy. By relying solely on continuous surface features (curvature and depth), the relevance of this pre-training is confirmed by the model's ability to detect localized and rare topological traits, such as sulcal interruptions. The downstream labeling task, however, introduces extracted sulcal fundi (lines) as an explicit semantic input. To bridge this dimensional domain gap (from purely geometric to semantic) without causing catastrophic forgetting, these anatomical lines are integrated into the pre-trained backbone via a soft-initialized Topological Prior Injector.
  Our experiments demonstrate that this approach outperforms fully supervised baselines trained from scratch, achieving a mean Dice of 0.77. Crucially, a local analysis reveals that the self-supervised geometric priors yield the largest performance gains on variable and tertiary sulci (up to 14.8%), confirming that learning the cortex shape is highly beneficial for identifying its rarest parts.

</details>


### [194] [Explaining Time Series Forecasting with Horizon-Resolved Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12639)
*Seunghan Lee,Jun Seo,Jaehoon Lee,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HRX（Horizon-Resolved eXplanation），为时间序列预测模型的解释增加"预测步"维度，使每个预测步拥有独立的重要性图，指出不同预测步依赖不同历史值。HRX是即插即用框架，含估计器、评估协议与秩判据三部分，并发现该逐步依赖具有低维性，实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列模型解释方法大多只返回单一重要性向量，假设每个预测步依赖相同的历史值。作者指出该假设并不成立，不同预测步实际依赖不同的过去值，因此需要引入"预测步"维度来获得更精确的解释。

Method: 提出HRX框架，包含三部分：1）估计器，可在不修改时间序列主干网络的前提下从任意可微预测器中读取这些重要性图；2）评估协议，通过移除重要性图排名最高的输入来衡量单一步预测的变化，以验证"预测步"维度；3）秩判据，提前判断在给定时间序列上是否值得解析该维度。此外揭示逐步依赖的低维性，所有步的解释由少数共享图构成，数量不随预测长度增长。

Result: 在多种主干网络和数据集上的大量实验表明，性能提升来源于"预测步"维度，且该结论对已有解释方法的估计器同样成立。

Conclusion: HRX证明了不同预测步依赖不同历史值，通过增加预测步维度可显著提升时间序列模型解释的有效性与准确性，且该依赖结构具有低维、可共享的特性，方法通用且可即插即用。代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in explaining time series (TS) models have produced methods that identify which past values a prediction depends on. However, most existing methods return a single importance vector, assuming that every predicted step depends on the same past values. In this paper, we show that this assumption does not hold, as different forecast steps depend on different past values. Motivated by this observation, we propose Horizon-Resolved eXplanation (HRX), which adds a horizon axis to the explanation, so that every forecast step receives its own importance map. HRX is a simple yet effective plug-in framework with three components: 1) an estimator that reads these maps out of any differentiable forecaster without modifying the TS backbone, 2) an evaluation protocol that validates the horizon axis by measuring how much a single forecast step changes when the inputs an importance map ranks highest are removed, and 3) a rank criterion that predicts in advance whether the axis is worth resolving on a given TS. We further show that this step-wise dependence is low-dimensional, as the explanations of all steps are built from a few shared maps whose number does not grow with the forecast length. Extensive experiments across various backbones and datasets show that the improvement comes from the horizon axis and holds for estimators of previous explanation methods. Code is available at https://github.com/seunghan96/HRX.

</details>


### [195] [Distortion of AI Alignment Revisited: RLHF is a Decent Utilitarian Aligner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12651)
*Kazusato Oko,Annie Ulichney,Nika Haghtalab,Han Bao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对带奖励裁剪的RLHF的失真进行细粒度分析，证明指数级退化并非RLHF算法的固有性质，而是偏好数据分布μ与KL参考策略π_ref之间分布不匹配造成的，并给出跨不同KL正则强度区间的紧致上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF是使大语言模型对齐人类偏好的标准范式，但在用户偏好异质的多元场景下其有效性受到质疑。近期工作（Gölz et al., 2025）表明，当用户偏好异质时，RLHF策略的平均用户效用与最优平均效用之间的乘性差距（失真）会随Bradley-Terry温度参数β指数增长。本文旨在探究这种指数退化是否真的是RLHF算法的根本属性。

Method: 作者对带奖励裁剪的RLHF的失真展开细粒度理论分析，在KL正则化强度的多个区间上建立了失真的紧致上界与下界，并将失真与偏好数据分布μ和KL参考策略π_ref之间的分布不匹配（以对数密度比上界B刻画）联系起来。

Result: 在一个代表性区间内，Bradley-Terry模型下失真为Õ(βB + β)，其中B是μ与π_ref之间对数密度比的上界。特别地，当不存在分布不匹配（即μ=π_ref）时，RLHF可达到最优失真O(β)（至多相差常数）。

Conclusion: 指数级退化并非RLHF算法的固有属性，而是分布不匹配的结果。因此，若想合理最大化平均效用，最好使用同策略采样的偏好数据，或在来源接近μ的数据上于RLHF之前进行微调。

Abstract: While Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) is the standard paradigm for aligning large language models with human preferences, its effectiveness in pluralistic settings has been called into question. Notably, recent work by Gölz et al. (2025) demonstrated that the \textit{distortion} -- defined as the multiplicative gap between the average user utility of the RLHF policy and the optimal average utility -- can scale exponentially with the Bradley-Terry temperature parameter $β$ when users have heterogeneous preferences. In this work, we present a fine-grained analysis of the distortion of RLHF with reward clipping and demonstrate that such exponential degradation is not a fundamental property of the algorithm but rather a consequence of distribution mismatch between the distribution generating preference data ($μ$) and the KL reference policy ($π_{\mathrm{ref}}$). To this end, we establish tight upper and lower bounds on the distortion of RLHF across multiple regimes of the KL regularization strength. We show that in a representative regime, under the Bradley-Terry model, the distortion is $\tildeΘ(βB + β)$, where $B$ is an upper bound on the log density ratio between $μ$ and $π_{\mathrm{ref}}$. In particular, when there is no distribution mismatch (i.e., $μ= π_{\mathrm{ref}}$), RLHF achieves the optimal distortion of $O(β)$ up to a constant. Our results suggest that, to reasonably maximize average utility with RLHF, it is preferable to use on-policy sampled preference data or to fine-tune before RLHF on data from a source close to $μ$.

</details>


### [196] [ProactiveBench: Can Streaming Video Models Really Interact Like Humans?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12658)
*Kaixuan Du,Xin Wan,YuKun Wang,Hang Zhang,Meng Cao,Dai Guan,Ming Chen,Ni Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ProactiveBench 基准，评估流式视频理解模型在无显式响应提示下主动监测并适时响应的能力，包含六个子任务，发现多数系统过早响应多于漏响应，暴露时间决策能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频理解评测多为被动反应式：在选定时间戳查询模型，无法评估模型"何时该响应"。主动交互需要模型持续监测常驻请求，在目标事件后的合适区间内响应，否则保持沉默。

Method: 构建 ProactiveBench，以一秒为间隔评估流式输入且不提供显式响应提示。设计六个子任务，变化触发条件的模糊性与时间容差：事件敏感性（在同一段录制上几何式结合响应率与沉默率）、四个基于窗口的子任务（区分提前、窗口内和遗漏响应）、重复计数（惩罚遗漏与重复）。

Result: 在六个被评估系统中，有四个系统出现提前响应多于遗漏响应的情况，显示其时间决策存在明显差距。

Conclusion: 主动交互评测揭示了现有模型在类人交互所需时间决策上的显著不足，ProactiveBench 为此提供了细粒度评估手段。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires models to process continuous multimodal input while maintaining temporal context. Existing evaluations are predominantly reactive: they query a model at a selected timestamp and therefore do not assess when it should respond. Proactive interaction instead requires monitoring a standing request, responding within an appropriate interval after the target event, and otherwise remaining silent. We introduce ProactiveBench, which evaluates models at one-second stream intervals without an explicit response cue. Its six subtasks vary trigger ambiguity and timing tolerance. Event Sensitivity geometrically combines response and silence rates on the same recording; four window-based subtasks distinguish early, in-window, and missed responses; and Duplicate Counting penalizes omissions and repetitions. Premature responses outnumber missed responses for four of the six evaluated systems, revealing a substantial gap in the temporal decision-making required for human-like interaction.

</details>


### [197] [SIFPBPNet: A Dual-Path Network for Wearable and Cuffless Blood Pressure Estimation via Individualized Steady-state Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12690)
*Shuailong Tang,Xiaoyu Li,Donglin Xie,Wei Chen,Guangpu Zhu,Yelei Li,Yali Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双路径架构SIFPBPNet，分离稳态与瞬时特征，实现无袖带连续血压监测，性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于PPG的无袖带连续血压监测对低成本、个性化心血管健康管理具有重要价值。然而，人群异质性以及"一对多映射"问题（不同个体的相似波形对应不同血压水平）限制了传统群体模型的准确性。

Method: 提出双路径架构SIFPBPNet，分别表示稳态与瞬时特征。稳态特征路径（SFP）采用图注意力网络（GAT）从多日历史PPG轨迹中提取个体特异性的长期特征；瞬时特征路径（IFP）从当前PPG片段捕捉短期动态，并通过交叉注意力机制引入稳态先验。

Result: 在大规模可穿戴数据集上，SIFPBPNet的收缩压和舒张压MAE分别为8.57和5.97 mmHg，优于现有最优模型。SFP模块集成到多种骨干架构时均能提升性能，收缩压相对MAE降低2.8%-13.1%。

Conclusion: SFP模块具有良好的泛化性与即插即用可迁移性，在精准无袖带血压监测方面具有较大潜力。

Abstract: Continuous and cuffless blood pressure (BP) monitoring using photoplethysmography (PPG) is of great interest for low-cost and personalized cardiovascular health management. However, significant population heterogeneity and the "one-to-many mapping" problem, where similar waveforms across individuals correspond to different BP levels, limit the accuracy of conventional population-based models. To address this challenge, we propose a dual-path architecture termed SIFPBPNet, which separately represents steady-state and instantaneous features, through a Steady-state Feature Path (SFP) and an Instantaneous Feature Path (IFP). The SFP employs a Graph Attention Network (GAT) to extract individual-specific and long-term characteristics from multi-day historical PPG trajectories. In parallel, the IFP captures short-term dynamics from current PPG segments and incorporates the steady-state prior via a cross-attention mechanism. Experiments on a large-scale wearable dataset demonstrate that SIFPBPNet achieves a Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 8.57 and 5.97 mmHg for systolic and diastolic BP, respectively, outperforming state-of-the-art models. Furthermore, the SFP module consistently improves performance when integrated into various backbone architectures, yielding 2.8-13.1% relative MAE reductions for systolic BP. These results highlight the strong generalizability and plug-and-play transferability of the SFP module, underscoring its great potential for accurate cuffless BP monitoring.

</details>


### [198] [Write on Paper and Get the Online Digital Trace:\newline A New Era for Handwriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12702)
*Florent Imbert,Yann Soullard,Eric Anquetil,Tanja Harbaum,Alexey Serdyuk,Fabian Kress,Tim Hamann,Peter Kampf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合智能数字笔、先进AI算法与自适应AI技术的创新方案，在不依赖外部参考系统的情况下，实时重建纸面手写的数字轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在普通纸上书写具有丰富触感、无延迟且有助于信息记忆，但现有的数字化手写方案（如电容屏、EMR数位板、特制纸张）均需专用笔或兼容基材，缺乏低成本、易推广且有效的解决方案。核心难题在于：在无外部参考系统、且允许笔在表面自由移动的前提下，仍能精确追踪笔的轨迹。

Method: 提出融合硬件与软件的方案：硬件上开发内置传感器的数字笔；软件上采用先进AI算法与自适应AI技术，并借助嵌入式AI实现轨迹重建与处理的实时优化。

Result: 摘要中未给出具体实验结果或量化指标，仅说明该工作旨在推动手写轨迹自动重建的技术前沿，实现传统纸面书写与数字轨迹捕捉的无缝衔接。

Conclusion: 该研究致力于突破纸面手写数字化的技术瓶颈，通过软硬件协同与嵌入式AI实现无外部参考的实时轨迹重建，从而连接传统书写与数字捕捉。

Abstract: Capturing the digital trace of handwriting usually requires a specific stylus and a compatible substrate, be it a capacitive touchscreen, an ElectroMagnetic Resonance (EMR) tablet as used in Wacom systems or special paper. While writing on regular paper offers rich haptics, no latency and is well known for improving information retention, no low-cost and widely accepted, effective solution exists to digitize such a pen trace. The challenge is to accurately track the pen's trajectory without an external reference system while allowing unrestricted freedom of pen movement across a surface. We propose an innovative solution that combines a digital pen, advanced artificial intelligence algorithms, and adaptive AI techniques to reconstruct the digital trace of handwriting. Our approach integrates hardware development, focusing on a sensor-equipped pen, with software innovations to optimize trajectory reconstruction and processing in real time using an embedded AI. This work aims to advance the state-of-the-art in automated trace reconstruction of handwriting, enabling a seamless connection between traditional handwriting on paper and capturing the trace digitally.

</details>


### [199] [InRTL: Effective Intra-Inter Interaction Learning for Relational Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12712)
*Weichen Li,Ken Zhong,Zheng Wang,Li Pan,Jianhua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的InRTL框架，显式建模关系表内部与表间依赖，并通过线性化注意力和异构图神经网络提升可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系表学习是建模通过主键-外键（PK-FK）关系连接的多表的重要方向，但目前仍缺乏针对该任务的原则性建模框架。

Method: InRTL形式化两类互补交互模式：表内交互（同一表内行间关联）和表间交互（跨PK-FK连接表的行间依赖）。采用列感知表编码器生成初始行表示，并用基于Transformer的自注意力和交叉注意力分别学习表内和表间依赖；进一步提升可扩展性时，引入线性化注意力和异构图神经网络简化自注意力与交叉注意力操作。

Result: 在覆盖24个真实任务的十个数据集上进行的大量实验验证了方法的有效性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: InRTL为关系表学习提供了统一且可扩展的建模框架，能同时捕捉表内和表间依赖，并具有较好的实际效果。

Abstract: Relational table learning has recently emerged as an important research direction for modeling multiple tables connected through primary key-foreign key (PK-FK) relationships. Despite recent advances, a principled modeling framework tailored to this task remains underexplored. In this paper, we propose Intra-Inter Relational Table Learning (InRTL), a unified framework that explicitly models dependencies both within and across relational tables. Specifically, InRTL formalizes two complementary interaction patterns: intra-table interactions, describing associations among rows within the same table, and inter-table interactions, describing dependencies between rows across PK-FK-linked tables. To model these dependencies, we develop a column-aware table encoder to generate initial row representations, followed by Transformer-based self-attention and cross-attention modules for intra-table and inter-table learning, respectively. To further improve scalability, InRTL incorporates linearized attention and heterogeneous graph neural networks to simplify the self-attention and cross-attention operations. Extensive experiments on ten datasets covering 24 real-world tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. Code is available at https://github.com/W1nterFloW/InRTL.

</details>


### [200] [Physics-Guided Synthetic High-Frequency Ultrasound Generation for Skin Layer Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12735)
*Junkyung ju,Kyungho Yoon,Minwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理引导的合成高频超声(HFUS)生成框架，用k-Wave构建多层皮肤声学幻影生成配对图像与密集掩膜，用于皮肤分层分割预训练，在真实数据微调后四分之三架构的Dice/IoU提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HFUS可无创可视化浅表皮肤结构，但自动化皮肤分层分析受限于密集标注数据的稀缺。现有真实HFUS数据集通常只标注表皮、表皮下低回声带等浅层目标，而真皮、皮下组织、筋膜、肌肉等深层结构的密集标签几乎不可得，因此需要新的数据获取途径。

Method: 构建多层声学皮肤幻影，为各层分配依赖层级的声学属性，利用k-Wave仿真生成配对的合成HFUS图像、密集层掩膜与仿真元数据；随后将该合成数据用于下游分割预训练，并在真实Mendeley HFUS数据上微调模型。

Result: 合成预训练+真实微调在真实域的性能与仅用真实数据训练相当，并在四个可训练架构中的三个上提升了平均Dice/IoU。

Conclusion: 物理引导的合成HFUS图像包含可迁移的解剖与纹理线索，可用于真实域皮肤分层分割；但仍需进一步缩小合成-真实外观差距以取得更大增益。代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: High-frequency ultrasound (HFUS) enables noninvasive visualization of superficial skin structures, but automated skin-layer analysis is limited by the scarcity of densely annotated data. Existing real HFUS datasets commonly provide annotations for superficial targets such as the epidermis and subepidermal low-echogenic band (SLEB), while dense labels for deeper structures such as dermis, subcutaneous tissue, fascia, and muscle are rarely available. We propose a physics-guided synthetic HFUS generation framework for skin layer segmentation. The framework constructs multilayer acoustic skin phantoms, assigns layer dependent acoustic properties, and uses k-Wave simulation to generate paired synthetic HFUS images, dense layer masks, and simulation metadata. To evaluate whether the generated data provide transferable supervision, we use it for downstream segmentation pretraining and fine-tune the models on real Mendeley HFUS data. Synthetic pretraining followed by real fine-tuning achieved real-domain performance comparable to real-only training and improved mean Dice/IoU in three of four evaluated trainable architectures. These results suggest that physics-guided synthetic HFUS images contain transferable anatomical and textural cues for real-domain skin layer segmentation, although further reduction of the synthetic-real appearance gap is needed to enable greater gains. The code and data are available at: https://github.com/Finn-02/synthetic-hfus-skin-layer-segmentation.

</details>


### [201] [Optimizing for the decision not the prediction: an exploration of Smooth Net Benefit as a training objective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12752)
*Koen M. F. Gorgels,Lasai Barreñada,Maarten van Smeden,Ben Van Calster,Ewout W. Steyerberg,Wouter A. C. van Amsterdam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并检验了一种光滑化的净获益（σNB）训练目标，用于让模型训练贴合特定阈值下的临床决策效用。基于Framingham数据及44个TabZilla数据集的72个"数据集-阈值"组合实验表明，σNB效果高度依赖情境：逻辑回归有微弱提升，GAM下降，XGBoost几乎无增益，因此不支持将其作为NLL的通用替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测模型通常以Bernoulli负对数似然（NLL）等目标训练，但下游临床决策往往取决于特定风险阈值。训练目标与决策效用之间存在错位，因此作者希望引入一种与阈值相关临床效用对齐的可微训练目标。

Method: 提出σNB——净获益（Net Benefit）的可微近似。将其作为训练目标应用于逻辑回归、广义可加模型（GAM）与XGBoost（含三种Hessian实现）。实验采用Framingham心血管风险数据集，以及44个TabZilla数据集共72个"数据集-阈值"组合进行评测。

Result: Framingham数据上σNB训练未能稳定提升净获益。TabZilla基准中，逻辑回归的平均标准化净获益由NLL的0.5669升至σNB的0.5765（平均差0.0096，95% CI -0.0001至0.0193）；GAM由0.5921降至0.5625（平均差-0.0296，95% CI -0.0721至0.0129）；XGBoost中NLL为0.6745，各σNB实现为0.6723--0.6735。逻辑回归中σNB的增益与XGBoost相对NLL训练逻辑回归的性能优势正相关。

Conclusion: σNB的效果具有情境依赖性：增益主要体现在模型灵活性较低的逻辑回归上，对更灵活模型类几乎无益。这表明当模型灵活性受限、改进空间较大时，决策导向优化可能最为有用。结果不支持将σNB作为NLL训练的通用替代，但支持在似然训练难以充分捕捉决策相关结构的场景中继续研究决策导向目标。

Abstract: Objective Prediction models are commonly trained using objectives such as Bernoulli negative log-likelihood (NLL), although downstream clinical decisions may depend on specific risk thresholds. We introduce Smooth Net Benefit ($σ$NB), a differentiable approximation of Net Benefit designed to align model training with threshold-specific clinical utility.
  Materials and Methods We evaluated $σ$NB as a training objective for logistic regression, generalized additive models (GAMs), and XGBoost with three Hessian implementations. Experiments used the Framingham cardiovascular risk dataset and 44 TabZilla datasets comprising 72 dataset-threshold combinations.
  Results $σ$NB training did not consistently improve Net Benefit in Framingham. Across the TabZilla benchmark, mean standardized Net Benefit for logistic regression increased from 0.5669 with NLL to 0.5765 with $σ$NB (mean difference 0.0096, 95% CI -0.0001 to 0.0193). For GAMs, mean standardized Net Benefit decreased from 0.5921 to 0.5625 (mean difference -0.0296, 95% CI -0.0721 to 0.0129). For XGBoost, NLL achieved 0.6745 compared with 0.6723--0.6735 across $σ$NB implementations. In logistic regression, $σ$NB gains were positively associated with the performance advantage of XGBoost over NLL-trained logistic regression.
  Discussion The effect of $σ$NB was context dependent, with modest gains concentrated in logistic regression and little benefit for more flexible model classes. This suggests that decision-focused optimization may be most useful when limited model flexibility leaves greater scope for improvement.
  Conclusion Our results do not support $σ$NB as a general replacement for NLL training, but support further investigation of decision-focused objectives in settings where conventional likelihood-based training may not adequately capture decision-relevant structure.

</details>


### [202] [Curriculum-Based Adversarial Heterogeneous Agent Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Quad-Copter Landing in Maritime Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12758)
*Allan Minh-Tam Nguyen,Sree Showrya Kotala,Stefan Banioi-Crijman,Kurt Driessens,Rico Möckel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究船载机械臂在空中捕获四旋翼无人机的问题，采用HAPPO强化学习并结合课程学习与对抗性风扰动智能体（HARL-AC）训练鲁棒协同控制策略，在分布外海况下取得更优泛化与更低坠毁率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上环境中回收无人机面临风湍流与甲板运动的挑战，传统着陆方法往往失效，因此需要探索替代的控制与学习方法。空中捕获是一个有价值的测试案例。

Method: 在NVIDIA Isaac Lab中，使用HAPPO强化学习，结合课程学习与对抗性风扰动智能体（HARL-AC）训练异构多智能体的协同控制策略，并与基于课程的域随机化策略以及仅在单一海况下训练的基准策略进行对比。

Result: 在分布内海况0/4/5下，HARL-AC与域随机化表现相当，成功率高达97.5%。在分布外海况7/8/10下，HARL-AC泛化更优，海况10时中位成功率高出16%，坠毁率降低最多14%。对抗训练策略行为更谨慎，超时率仅增加不到3%。

Conclusion: 对抗性训练使策略在严重且未见过的海况下表现出更安全的回收行为，验证了HARL-AC方法在海上无人机空中捕获任务中的鲁棒性与泛化优势。

Abstract: Recovering unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) in maritime environments is challenging due to wind turbulence and ship-deck motion, making it a valuable test case for alternative control and learning approaches as conventional landing approaches often become unreliable. We study simulated mid-air capture of quadrotor UAVs by a ship-mounted robotic arm, learning robust cooperative control policies with Heterogeneous-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (HAPPO) Reinforcement Learning. We train with HAPPO using a curriculum and an adversarial wind agent (HARL-AC) in NVIDIA Isaac Lab, and compare the obtained control policies against those generated through curriculum-based domain randomization and a benchmark trained on a single sea state. In-distribution evaluation on sea states $0/4/5$ shows comparable success for HARL-AC and domain randomization of up to $97.5\%$. On out-of-distribution sea states $7/8/10$, HARL-AC generalizes better, achieving up to $16\%$ higher median success rate at sea state 10, and substantially lower crash rates of up to $14\%$ compared to the domain randomization policy. Furthermore, we show that the adversarially trained policy shows more cautious behavior, slightly increasing timeouts by $<3\%$, but yields safer recovery behavior in severe, unseen conditions.

</details>


### [203] [Convergence of Stochastic Gradient Methods under Heavy-Tailed Noise and Hölder Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12785)
*Misbah Uz Zaman,Anirbit Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究非凸随机优化在Hölder连续梯度与重尾噪声联合松弛假设下的收敛性，为SGD、δ-GClip和G-Clip给出收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典随机梯度方法收敛保证通常假设Lipschitz光滑目标和有限方差梯度噪声，这两点在实际中常被违反，因此需要在更弱假设下研究收敛性。

Method: 在目标具有(L,s)-Hölder连续梯度（s∈(0,1]）、梯度噪声仅满足有界α阶矩（α∈(1,2]）的联合松弛假设下，分别分析标准SGD、δ-正则化梯度裁剪（δ-GClip）和标准梯度裁剪（G-Clip）三种方法。

Result: 标准SGD在α≥1+s时收敛率为O(T^{-s/(1+s)})，同时推广至重尾噪声与Hölder光滑；δ-GClip在同一条件下得到平稳性速率O(T^{-2s(α-1)/[(1+s)(2α-1)]})；G-Clip在α≥1+s时达到上述速率，在非常重尾的α<1+s区域取得O(T^{-2s(α-1)/[(α-1)+s(2α-1)]})。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典非凸SGD收敛结果扩展到Hölder光滑与重尾噪声并存的场景，并为梯度裁剪方法提供了理论保证，其中G-Clip在α<1+s的极重尾情形下的结果为该领域首个基于随机梯度方法的收敛保证。

Abstract: Classical convergence guarantees for stochastic gradient methods typically assume Lipschitz-smooth objectives and finite-variance gradient noise, both frequently violated in practice. In contrast, we study nonconvex stochastic optimization under the joint relaxation of these assumptions: objectives with $(L,s)$-Hölder continuous gradients, $s\in(0,1]$, and gradient noise satisfying only a bounded $α$-th moment condition for $α\in(1,2]$. We establish three convergence results. Firstly, that standard SGD converges at rate $O(T^{-s/(1+s)})$ whenever $α\ge1+s$, extending the classical nonconvex SGD rate to heavy-tailed noise and Hölder smoothness simultaneously. Secondly, we analyze $δ$-regularized gradient clipping ($δ$-GClip), a provable trainer of wide and deep nets, and establish a stationarity rate of $O(T^{-2s(α-1)/[(1+s)(2α-1)]})$ under the same condition. Thirdly, we analyze standard gradient clipping (G-Clip) and show that it recovers the above rate for $α\ge1+s$ while in the very heavy-tailed regime $α<1+s$, it has a convergence rate $O(T^{-2s(α-1)/[(α-1)+s(2α-1)]})$ --- the first convergence guarantee in this regime for any stochastic gradient based method.

</details>


### [204] [VertiFuseX: Generalizable Financial Forecasting via Multi-Stream Temporal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12793)
*Aashish Bohra,Vivek Vijay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VertiFuseX混合LSTM架构，通过倒数第二层垂直融合多尺度时序特征，在10个全球股指15年数据上实现显著的股价预测性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列具有非平稳性和高噪声特征，现有深度学习模型依赖僵化的决策级融合、临时性超参数调优以及压缩的末层输出，导致信息损失、过拟合和跨市场泛化能力有限。

Method: VertiFuseX采用倒数第二层垂直融合策略，堆叠并重新加权来自LSTM、Bi-LSTM和St-LSTM分支的倒数第二层特征，同时集成一个并行DNN流，在固定超参数配置下通过反向传播联合优化所有组件，从而保留更丰富的多尺度中间时序信息。

Result: 在10个全球股指2010-2024年收盘价数据上（保留最后365个交易日作为样本外测试），相较LSTM基线MAPE降低30-54%，MAE和RMSE改善超40%，在33项指标-数据集比较中优于七个先进模型。消融实验证实倒数第二层融合优于末层融合与决策级集成；梯度显著性分析显示模型持续关注9-15天滞后的中期依赖；极端市场行情下的算法交易模拟显示最大回撤降低且风险调整收益更优。

Conclusion: VertiFuseX仅需675k参数、2.6MB内存占用和1.5ms/样本推理延迟，为稳健金融预测提供了一个轻量、可解释、可部署的框架。

Abstract: Stock price prediction remains challenging due to the non-stationary and noisy nature of financial time series. Existing deep learning models often rely on rigid decision-level fusion, ad hoc hyperparameter tuning, and compressed final-layer outputs, causing information loss, overfitting, and limited cross-market generalization. We propose VertiFuseX, a hybrid LSTM architecture using penultimate-layer vertical fusion of multi-scale temporal representations. VertiFuseX stacks and reweights penultimate features from LSTM, Bi-LSTM, and St-LSTM branches, integrates a parallel DNN stream, and jointly optimizes all components via backpropagation under a fixed hyperparameter configuration. This preserves richer intermediate temporal information across scales. Evaluated on 15 years (2010-2024) of closing prices from 10 global equity indices using strict chronological out-of-sample testing with the final 365 trading days held out, VertiFuseX achieves 30-54% MAPE reductions and over 40% improvements in MAE and RMSE versus LSTM-based baselines, and outperforms seven state-of-the-art models across 33 metric-dataset comparisons. Ablation studies confirm penultimate-layer fusion drives these gains over final-layer fusion and decision-level ensembling. Gradient-based saliency analysis shows consistent emphasis on mid-range dependencies at lags 9-15 days. Economic validation via algorithmic trading simulation under extreme market regimes shows reduced maximum drawdowns and superior risk-adjusted returns. With 675k parameters, a 2.6 MB memory footprint, and 1.5 ms/sample inference latency, VertiFuseX offers a lightweight, interpretable, deployment-ready framework for robust financial forecasting.

</details>


### [205] [GenOR-Twin: A Semantic Middleware for Integrating Operational Discourse with Mathematical Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12863)
*Rahimeh Neamatian Monemi,Shahin Gelareh,Lubin Cui,Nelson Maculan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GenOR-Twin神经符号框架，将LLM定位为语义翻译器而非直接求解器，通过动态约束注入实现实时优化，并在六个优化领域验证泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在弥合非结构化运维日志与严格数学优化之间的翻译鸿沟。核心痛点是：LLM直接求解会丧失精确组合方法的可行性保证，而传统优化又难以处理定性的人类输入和真实环境的不确定性，因此需要一个能兼顾语义理解与数学严谨性的桥接框架。

Method: 构建神经符号框架GenOR-Twin：将LLM用作语义翻译器而非直接求解器；设计动态约束注入机制，把定性的中断事件在运行时翻译为形式化数学约束，实时修改优化问题的可行域；通过运维观测更新虚拟模型状态、优化决策回写知识图谱，实现双向耦合以满足数字孪生的同步要求；并引入自适应决策策略，根据系统可用松弛量在低复杂度计划修复与完全重优化之间自动选择。

Result: 在六个不同优化领域上验证了方法的泛化性；系统保留了精确组合方法的可行性保证，并能够将静态模型转化为可适应真实环境运行不确定性与变异性的韧性系统。

Conclusion: GenOR-Twin通过神经符号结合的方式证明了LLM可以在优化系统中扮演语义翻译角色而非求解器角色，从而在保持数学严谨性的同时引入对人类定性输入的实时响应能力，为构建真正的数字孪生型优化系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce GenOR-Twin, a neuro-symbolic framework that bridges the translation gap between unstructured operational logs and rigorous mathematical optimization. Our architecture uniquely positions Large Language Models as semantic translators rather than direct solvers, ensuring that the system retains the feasibility guarantees of exact combinatorial methods. { \color{red}We design a dynamic constraint injection mechanism (the runtime translation of qualitative disruption events into formal mathematical constraints) that allows the system to structurally modify the optimization problem's feasibility region in real-time based on qualitative human inputs. The resulting bidirectional coupling---where operational observations update the virtual model state and optimized decisions are reflected back into the Knowledge Graph---satisfies the synchronization requirement of a proper Digital Twin. The framework features an adaptive decision policy} that automatically selects between low-complexity schedule repair and full re-optimization by analyzing the available system slack. Finally, we demonstrate the generalization of this approach across six distinct optimization domains, {\color{red}turning static models into resilient systems that adapt to the operational uncertainty and variability of real-world environments.}

</details>


### [206] [What an odour descriptor corpus can and cannot measure: valence, attenuation, and the ceiling of the public record](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12875)
*Stylianos Kampakis,Fabio Rovai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文审计了Pyrfume的四个气味描述符语料库，发现语料库间存在异质性描述符效应（I²=80%），无法通过简单偏移修复；结构指纹和描述符标签最多仅达到人类可靠性可及上限的约1/3，缺失的方差主要来自"效价"（愉悦度），需通过人类评级直接测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器嗅觉普遍基于合并的公共描述符语料库进行训练，但"共享的描述符词在不同语料库中是否测量同一事物"从未被检验，且任何语料库所能测量能力的上限也未知。作者希望系统审计语料库间的一致性，并探明描述符和结构信息能够达到的测量天花板。

Method: 以分子为条件，用McNemar检验作为语料库效应的精确条件检验，审计Pyrfume的四个语料库；用I²量化异质性，用四相关（tetrachoric）一致性和κ系数比较来源差异；用ETS量表评估差异功能（DIF）；以人类评审小组的可靠性作为可及上限，检验Morgan指纹加完整RDKit描述符块、合并两个语料库的全部标签的效果；并测试引入效价（愉悦度）评级的表现。

Result: 语料库在描述符上异质不一致（I²=80%），并随标注广度非均匀变化（z=17.2），单一偏移无法修复合并；四相关中位数一致性0.795而κ中位数仅0.413；109个可估计效应的描述符中36个在ETS量表上显示大差异功能。Morgan指纹加完整RDKit描述符块达到可及上限的32.9%，合并两语料库全部标签达33.9%，且差距不因模型容量、编码选择、更多分子或更多词而缩小。加入每分子一次愉悦度评级达54.6%（独立旧仪器57.1%）；从描述符恢复的效价（ρ=0.457）仅得16.6%。五个评审者超过结构加完整描述符记录，十五到二十个评审者达到饱和。

Conclusion: 缺失的方差来自效价，必须被直接测量而非从描述符推断；描述符标签和结构信息的天花板远低于人类可靠性可及上限；作者发布了一个描述符交叉映射表和二十个机器验证定理，指出当前机器嗅觉方法的核心局限，并强调人类受体评级在效价维度上的不可替代性。

Abstract: Machine olfaction trains on pooled public descriptor corpora, but whether a shared descriptor word measures the same thing across corpora has not been tested, nor has the ceiling of what any of them can measure.
  We audit four corpora from Pyrfume. Conditioning on the molecule makes McNemar's test the exact conditional test of the corpus effect. Corpora disagree heterogeneously across descriptors ($I^2 = 80\%$) and non-uniformly with labelling breadth ($z = 17.2$), so no single offset repairs pooling. Median tetrachoric agreement is 0.795 against median $κ$ of 0.413: sources largely concur on which molecules deserve a word and differ on how readily they apply it. Of 109 descriptors with an estimable effect, 36 show large differential functioning on the ETS scale.
  Against a human panel's reliability, Morgan fingerprints with the full RDKit descriptor block reach 32.9\% of achievable; adding every label from two merged corpora reaches 33.9\%. The gap does not close with model capacity, encoding choice, more molecules, or more words.
  The missing variance is valence. One pleasantness rating per molecule reaches 54.6\% of achievable (57.1\% on an independent older instrument). Valence recovered from descriptors ($ρ= 0.457$) yields only 16.6\%, so it must be measured. Five raters exceed structure plus the full descriptor record; fifteen to twenty saturate. We release a descriptor crosswalk and twenty machine-checked theorems.

</details>


### [207] [Large Distant Gradients Need Not Be Reliable: reliability-weighted credit assignment for long-horizon autoregressive forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12890)
*Junhao Zhao,David Michael Simberg,Jacob Kang,Colin Connor Kurniawan,Nan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Internal Dual-Wiener routing (Internal-DW)，一种仅作用于反向传播的干预方法，在保留完整前向 rollout 与全部 horizon 损失的同时，对内部梯度路径做可靠性加权，缓解 BPTT 中远距离梯度主导更新且信噪比下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归预测中长 rollout 提供远距离监督，但 BPTT 会沿多步传播梯度。反复的雅可比乘积使远距离梯度可能主导更新，并同时放大可预测信号与不可预测噪声，因此大的远距离梯度未必携带可靠学习信号。

Method: Internal-DW 在每个残差块为恒等路径与非线性路径推导有界 Wiener 增益，在保留可预测学习信号与抑制不可预测变化之间做权衡，并基于路径级梯度统计与显式噪声模型估计增益。它是一种 backward-only 干预，不改变前向 rollout 和 horizon 损失。

Result: 在已知梯度信噪比的受控系统中，远距离梯度可在 SNR 下降时仍增大；Internal-DW 降低了恢复可预测梯度信号的留出误差并改善预测。在四个“历史主导、弱驱动”测试集上，相对完整 BPTT 降低预测误差 5.2%-13.8%，在四个上均优于梯度裁剪与雅可比正则化，在三个上优于验证集选择的 TBPTT，并扩展或保持了最优训练 horizon 范围。但在整个基准套件中，当可用历史有限或所选采样器无法表征主要的驱动相关变化时，其收益会减弱甚至反转。

Conclusion: 保留长期 horizon 监督并不意味着必须同等信任每一个反向贡献；通过对内部梯度路径做可靠性加权，可以在不牺牲长程监督的前提下提升自回归预测的稳定性与准确度，但其适用性存在明确边界。

Abstract: In autoregressive forecasting, long prediction rollouts provide distant supervision, but backpropagation through time (BPTT) carries gradients from those losses through many autoregressive steps. Repeated Jacobian products can make distant gradients dominate the update while amplifying predictable signal and unpredictable noise together; a large distant gradient therefore need not carry reliable learning signal. Motivated by this observation, we introduce Internal Dual-Wiener routing (Internal-DW), a principled backward-only intervention that preserves the full forward rollout and all horizon losses while reliability-weighting internal gradient routes. At each residual block, we derive bounded Wiener gains for the identity and nonlinear routes that balance preserving predictable learning signal against suppressing unpredictable variation, and estimate them from route-level gradient statistics and an explicit noise model. In a controlled system with known gradient signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), we show that distant gradients can grow even as their SNR falls, and that Internal-DW reduces held-out error in recovering predictable gradient signals and improves forecasting. On four history-dominated, weak-drive testbeds, Internal-DW reduces forecast error by 5.2%-13.8% relative to full BPTT, outperforms gradient clipping and Jacobian regularization on all four, and outperforms validation-selected truncated BPTT (TBPTT) on three. It also extends or preserves the fitted optimal training-horizon range across these four testbeds. Across the full benchmark suite, the current Internal-DW estimator has a clear applicability boundary: its benefit diminishes or reverses when usable history is limited or when the selected sampler fails to represent dominant drive-dependent variation. The results show that retaining long-horizon supervision does not require trusting every backward contribution equally.

</details>


### [208] [Quantifying the Value of Privileged Information Using a PAC-Bayesian Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12891)
*Vasily Bokov,Sebastian Schmitt,Vedran Dunjko,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于PAC-Bayes的算法无关、信息论框架，用于量化特权信息（LUPI）可带来的潜在增益，并引入仅依赖训练风险的经验度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特权信息（PI）虽被期望提升模型性能，但Vapnik原始理论及后续工作多为特定算法和特定场景的证明，缺乏通用框架解释PI如何及何时传递有用知识。

Method: 基于PAC-Bayes框架，构建算法无关的信息论方法；通过比较有/无PI时最紧可达风险界，定义PI的潜在价值上限；提出可直接从经验训练风险估计该潜力的度量，无需测试时数据。

Result: 在监督与无监督设置中验证：训练阶段度量与真实测试阶段性能增益之间具有稳健的一致性。

Conclusion: 该工作为LUPI的信息论理解迈出必要一步，可在选定模型前量化特权特征潜力。

Abstract: In practice, various learning scenarios provide access to auxiliary features exclusively during training. Incorporating such data to enhance model performance gave rise to a paradigm known as Learning Using Privileged Information (LUPI). While this extra information is intended to improve the resulting model, establishing a generalized, cohesive understanding of how privileged information (PI) transfers useful knowledge remains a challenge. Vapnik's original theory and subsequent works offer performance guarantees in certain cases, but these results are inherently per-algorithm and rely on setting-specific proof approaches. Consequently, a more general framework explaining how and when PI transfers useful knowledge is still missing. To bridge this gap, we introduce an algorithm-agnostic, information-theoretic approach based on the PAC-Bayes framework. Rather than asking whether a particular algorithm exploits PI, we ask how much value it could offer: comparing the tightest achievable risk bound with and without PI yields its potential - an upper limit on the extractable gain. We introduce a metric that quantifies this potential directly from empirical training risk, bypassing the need for test-time data access, and validate our findings in both supervised and unsupervised settings. The results demonstrate a robust correspondence between our training-time metric and true test-time performance gains. Ultimately, this work takes a necessary step toward an information-theoretic understanding of LUPI, and quantifying the potential of privileged features before committing to a model.

</details>


### [209] [Behavior Quotient Learning for Low-Rank Adaptation of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12896)
*Pengyang Zhou,Xiaobin Tu,Zhengxi Liu,Rongkun Xue,Haochen Li,Miancan Liu,Ziyuan Chen,Yinggui Wang,Jinkui Ren,Xiantao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BQ-LoRA，通过行为商流形组织轨迹更新，用BQB平衡与DPC压缩解决单一LoRA学习多样智能体轨迹时的冗余更新与秩预算失真问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖异构交互能力完成复杂任务。现有方法将能力分散到多个LoRA适配器，增加存储并引入推理路由开销；单一LoRA虽避免开销，但在固定秩预算下从多样轨迹学习面临两大挑战：一是不同轨迹及参数梯度可能产生等价的决策分布变化，导致重复更新过度强调冗余行为变化；二是聚合更新可能超出适配器秩预算，而权重空间近似会扭曲其意图产生的决策变化。

Method: BQ-LoRA低秩适应框架，通过局部行为商流形组织轨迹更新，包含两个模块：行为商平衡（BQB）从决策分布构造商流形，根据轨迹更新方向在商切空间中的局部密度重加权其更新方向；决策保持压缩（DPC）将平衡后的梯度投影到内在固定秩切空间，并通过联合控制有效权重误差与决策分布失真来重构目标。

Result: 在AppWorld与BrowseComp-Plus上与标准LoRA及近期低秩适应方法进行对比实验，并通过单独消融评估两个组件的互补贡献。

Conclusion: BQ-LoRA以单一LoRA在固定秩预算下处理多样智能体轨迹，兼顾存储与推理效率，并通过行为商流形与决策保持压缩缓解冗余更新和决策失真。

Abstract: LLM-based agents rely on heterogeneous interaction capabilities to accomplish complex tasks. Existing approaches often distribute these capabilities across multiple LoRA adapters, which increases adapter storage requirements and introduces routing overhead during inference. A single LoRA avoids this overhead, but learning from diverse agent trajectories under a fixed rank budget presents two challenges. First, trajectories with different interaction traces and parameter gradients can induce equivalent changes in decision distributions, causing repeated updates to overemphasize redundant behavioral changes. Second, an aggregated update may exceed the rank budget of the adapter, and approximating it in weight space can distort the decision changes that it is intended to produce. We propose BQ-LoRA, a low-rank adaptation framework that organizes trajectory updates through a local behavior quotient manifold. It contains two modules, i.e., behavior quotient balancing (BQB) and decision preserving compression (DPC). BQB constructs the quotient manifold from decision distributions and reweights trajectory update directions according to their local density in the quotient tangent space. DPC projects the balanced gradient onto the intrinsic fixed rank tangent space and refactorizes the resulting target by jointly controlling effective weight error and distortion of decision distributions. Experiments on AppWorld and BrowseComp-Plus compare BQ-LoRA with standard LoRA and recent low-rank adaptation methods, while separate ablations evaluate the complementary contributions of both components.

</details>


### [210] [Physical-State-Guided Diffusion Sampling for Full-Waveform Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12899)
*Chen Min,Haowen Jiang,Zheng Ma,Xiongbin Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理状态引导扩散采样（PSG），通过高斯桥耦合持续物理速度与扩散先验，提升全波形反演稳定性与结构恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全波形反演（FWI）病态且非线性强，重建严重依赖初始化与先验；扩散后验采样虽提供地质先验，但直接与非线性波动方程求解器耦合会导致物理引导不可靠。

Method: PSG将持续物理速度通过高斯桥与扩散先验耦合：物理状态由波形拟合进行细化，并以去噪后的速度作正则化，反之引导反向扩散过程。该形式分离了波动方程梯度与去噪器梯度，同时保留了传统FWI初始化和累积优化历史。

Result: 在四个OpenFWI数据族上，PSG的终端去噪估计在干净与缺失道采集条件下均优于经典与扩散类基线，并在测量噪声下保持强结构恢复；重复随机运行保留主导地质结构，集合变异性集中于地质界面并与局部反演误差正相关。冻结的OpenFWI训练先验无需重训即可支持更大的Marmousi、Overthrust与BP2004 Salt模型反演。

Conclusion: PSG有效结合扩散先验与传统FWI优化，实现稳健且可迁移的地下速度反演。

Abstract: Full waveform inversion (FWI) estimates subsurface velocity from seismic recordings, but its ill-posedness and nonlinearity make accurate reconstruction strongly dependent on initialization and prior information. Diffusion posterior sampling provides a learned geological prior, yet directly coupling its denoiser to the nonlinear wave solver can yield unreliable physical guidance. We propose Physical-State-Guided Diffusion Sampling (PSG), which couples a persistent physical velocity to the diffusion prior through a Gaussian bridge. The physical state is refined by waveform fitting regularized by the denoised velocity, and in turn guides the reverse diffusion process. This formulation separates the wave-equation and denoiser gradients while preserving conventional FWI initialization and accumulated optimization history. On four OpenFWI families, PSG's terminal denoised estimates outperform classical and diffusion-based baselines under clean and missing-trace acquisitions and maintain strong structural recovery under measurement noise. Repeated stochastic runs preserve the dominant geological structures, with ensemble variability concentrated near geological interfaces and positively associated with local inversion error. A frozen OpenFWI-trained prior further supports inversion of the larger Marmousi, Overthrust, and BP2004 Salt models, recovering complex geological structures without retraining.

</details>


### [211] [Hidden in Rounds: Predicting the Time Cost of 802.11 Contention in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12903)
*Satwat Bashir,Tasos Dagiuklas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将ns-3测得的IEEE 802.11帧投递率作为FedAvg更新接入概率的一阶代理，用公式估计通信时间，在720次运行中考察客户端密度与负载对轮数与通信时间的影响，并用Bianchi模型锚定的估计器验证误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在IEEE 802.11上共享无线信道，不同客户端密度与负载会影响帧投递与通信时间，但已有研究较少系统量化这些无线因素对联邦训练收敛轮数与通信时间的影响。
